# Presents

_This article is about the gifts given to NPCs. Not to be confused with[Ornament Presents](/wiki/Ornament_Presents "Ornament Presents") and [Gift Boxes](/wiki/Gift_Boxes "Gift Boxes")._

Present

[![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/bee-swarm-simulator/images/0/02/Present.png/revision/latest?cb=20230404045255)

_"A festively wrapped gift. Exchange it with an NPC for prizes!"_

COOLDOWN

(None)

A **Present** is a Beesmas-exclusive [inventory item](/wiki/Category:Inventory "Category:Inventory") that has appeared in every [Beesmas](/wiki/Beesmas "Beesmas") event since 2020 following its debut in 2018 and hiatus in 2019. Presents can be given to most NPCs in the game in exchange for various prizes from the NPC. Each NPC can only receive one gift. 

To give a present, the player needs to talk to an NPC of their choice. When interacted with, special dialogue will appear, allowing the player to give the present to the chosen NPC. If the player does not have a present in their inventory or have already recently given said NPC a present, normal dialogue will display. Some NPCs may have other requirements for the player before it allows itself to receive a gift. 

  * 2025

  * 2024 (Winter)

  * 2024 (Summer)

  * 2022

  * 2021

  * 2020

  * 2018




Presents returned once again in Beesmas 2025. They still have their main function of adding [Ornaments](/wiki/Ornaments "Ornaments") to the [Beesmas Tree](/wiki/Beesmas_Tree "Beesmas Tree") when given to NPCs, though players can't give a present to [Sun Bear](/wiki/Sun_Bear "Sun Bear") as the bear is not present. 

## Ways to obtain

  * Multiple quests from [Bee Bear](/wiki/Bee_Bear "Bee Bear") award a total of ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)10 **Presents** , along with other rewards.
  * [Bee Bear’s Catalog](/wiki/Bee_Bear%E2%80%99s_Catalog "Bee Bear’s Catalog") sold ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 **Present** for ![Snowflake](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)250 [Snowflakes](/wiki/Snowflake "Snowflake") and ![Gingerbread Bear](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)10 [Gingerbread Bears](/wiki/Gingerbread_Bear "Gingerbread Bear").
  * [Onett](/wiki/Onett "Onett") gives ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 **Present** (along with other items) upon claiming a hive for the first time during the event's period.
  * Donating ![Strawberry](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Strawberries](/wiki/Strawberry "Strawberry") to the [Galentine Shrine](/wiki/Wind_Shrine#Galentine_Shrine "Wind Shrine") has a chance to give ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 **Present** , along with other rewards.
  * Completing [Robo Party](/wiki/Robo_Party "Robo Party")'s rank 25 for the first time gives ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 **Present** , along with other rewards.

## Dialogue

NPC  | Dialogue   
---|---  
Black Bear  | Oh, hello there! _-Present Given-_ What've you gotten me this year? I'll bet it's something special. ...(krumple krumple)... Wow! A honey-straw! A straw made specifically for drinking honey. I've heard so much about these. Somehow they get a lot mroe honey up in a lot less time. Thank you so much! Now here's your present: the [Honey Jar Ornament]! With this on the Beesmas Tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate+25% Capacity in the Sunflower Fieldand +5% Honey Per Pollen!Enjoy! And Happy Honeydays!   
Mother Bear  | The Honeydays bring the bees so much joy! _\--Present Given-_ Oh! Is this for me? How very sweet of you! Ill open it right now! ....(krumple krumple)... Aww! It's an evil little Snow Cub plushie! That Snow Cub is always getting up to no good, isn't it. If he were MY cub. Well, he'd probably be just as bad. I'm such a push over. Now go ahead and open up what i got for you! It's the [Stuffed Heart Ornament]! With this on the tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity in the Strawberry Field And +10% Pollen from Bee Gathering! Merry Beesmas! I hope your hive enjoys that!   
Brown Bear  | Brrrr, it's cold! _-Present Given-_ But not too cold for us to exchange gifts! So what's it, huh? What'd you get ol' Brown Bear? ...(krumple krumple)... Whoa! It's a Bwhoop health-tracking wrist band! It lets you know when your Royal Jelly levels are too low. And when your quest-stressing levels are too high! Thanks bud, this should be good for my health. Now look what i got you! The [Royal Jelly Ornament]! With that on the Beesmas Tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate. +25% Capacity in the Clover Field, and +20% Pollen from "Bomb" abilities! I'm gonna go calibrate this thing, Happy Beesmas!   
Panda Bear  | Hmm... _-Present Given-_ Even though I'm on guard, I'm still open to gifts. ...(krumple krumple)... WHOA! It's a Monster-Slay Offline Voucher! Onett told me he would NEVER add one of these things. Said we'd probably never see any of you again! Guess it slipped through the cracks. Thanks. Now you take this: the [Boxing Glove Ornament]. With that on the tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate. +25% Capacity in the Bamboo Field, and +5% Bee Attack Power! Stay safe out there, and Merry Beesmas.   
Science Bear  | Keeping busy on Beesmas, just like me, I presume. _-Present Given-_ Ah, busy getting me a gift I see! Let's get to the inspection. ...(krumple krumple)... Fascinating! This is a Beebini Honeycincincinappannini! A creature designed by an artificial intelligence no bear can comprehend. It appears to be half bee, and half panini. A miracle of science, to be sure. I'm not counting this as a bee discovery, though. Now it's your turn! I got you the [Conical Flask] ornament! With that on the Beesmas Tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity in the Pineapple Patch And +25% Convert Rate at the Hive! That ought to help make this Beesmas productive. Thanks again for all your help. Happy Honeydays! Now you'll excuse me, I'm going to take a DNA sample of this thing.   
Polar Bear  | It's good time of year [sic] to be a polar bear! _-Present Given-_ Especially for my cousin, Bee Bear. His entire identity is centered around Beesmas. At first our family thought it was weird, but we've become supportive over the years. Beesmas brings Bee Bear joy and gives him a sense of purpose. Who are we to judge? We all have our quirks. Me, I'm obsessed with cooking. So I hope you brought a cooking related gift! Come on, let's see what you brought. ...(krumple krumple)... Oh wow! A $50 gift card to Bearawhon! Don't know how far that'll go at such an expensive grocery store... Might get me a half pint of [Blueberries], or a single [Super Smoothie]. But I appreciate it! Now, go ahead and open yours. It's [Cast Iron Skillet] Ornament! With this on the tree, you'll receive these boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity in the Pumpkin Patch And +10% White Field Capacity! Hope that helps you complete my cousins [sic] crazy quests! Happy Honeydays!   
Dapper Bear  | You're free to warm yourself up in my shop. _-Present Given-_ A [Present] for me? What, do you like me or something? Well I hope so, cause I think quite highly of you. Let's see what you think I'd like. ...(krumple krumple)... Ah! A Sticker Book with all 400 slots unlocked! That must have cost a ton of [Tickets]. And for what? A little convenience? Heh, well, a little convenience goes a long way with me. Thank you. Now for you: It's the [Potpourri Ornament]. It's filled with flowers and leaves I grew in my Planters. The fragrance is pretty strong... but hey, smells like Beesmas. With this on the tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity in the Dandelion Field And +5% Nectar. I hope you'll get some use out of that. Because I've already got enough stickers to get use out of mine!   
Onett  | Is it snowing? I can't tell from in this... cave.. _-Present Given-_ A [Present] for me? Wow, thanks! Ok, let's see what it is. ...(krumple krumple)... Wow!! Some cough drops! Just what I needed. If this is because you've heard my voice crack... Well, I can explain. It does that when I'm tired and I've been talking a lot. A little embarrassing. But these will help! Ok, now look at what I made just for you... and everyone else who gives me a [Present]. It's the [Candy Cane Reindeer Ornament]! With this on the Beesmas tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity on the Mountain Top Field and +20% Tool Pollen! Oh, and some other things. Like the boosts you probably actually wanted. Merry Beesmas and Happy Happy Honeymaking!   
Spirit Bear  | Mmmm...mhmm...(she's murmuring to herself with her eyes close [sic])... _-Present Given-_ nil [sic]   
Robo Bear  | (click click click click click) _-Present Given-_ CHARITABLE GIFT DETECTED I am shocked and I am thankful and I will open this package now. >> Commencing Unwrapping Sequence ...(krumple krumple)... I've been gifted 32GB OF DDR5 RAM! With this, I'll be able to generate challenge rounds approximately 0.389% faster! I gladly accept this and offer you this in return: the [Digital Pet Ornament]! With that on the Beesmas Tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +10% Capacity +25% Capacity in the Spider Field +10% Pollen from Event Bees And +5% Pollen from Duped Abilities! >> GIFT EXCHANGE SEQUENCE COMPLETE: OUTCOME SUCCESS   
Gifted Riley Bee  | I should have built a roof like Bucko Bee... _-Present Given-_ WHAT! A GIFT? No way! Youuuuuuuu Well hand it over! Come on! ...(krumple krumple)... WHAT IS THIS????? I DIDN'T EVEN KNOW THEY EXISTED!! A 20% Red Pollen Hive Bonus Poinsettia!!! This is going to skyrocket the Red HQ into astronomical levels of wealth! Thank you thank you thank you! Now, mine's not quite that fancy, but here it is: It's the [Electric Candle Ornament]! With that on the Beesmas Tree, you'll receive these boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity in the Rose and Mushroom Field and +10% Pollen from Flames! Now to go look up what I could get for this thing I mean... to equip it and use it to it's [sic] fullest potential.   
Gifted Bucko Bee  | Looks chilly out there! _-Present Given-_ Well, if it isn't the coolest beekeeper around! You bringing me a [Present] makes you even cooler. Let's see what it is... ...(krumple krumple)... Well I'll be! A whole jar of marbles! Man... that's a lot of marbles. Some pretty cool ones in there. I see one that kind of looks like a [Swirled Wax]. That's neat. Can't wait to crack this open and roll those bad boys around. Thanks. You're truly cool. Take this, why don't you: The [Snowman Ornament]! With that on the Beesmas tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity in the Blue Flower Field and Pine Tree Forest and +10% Blue Field Capacity! Hope that helps your Blue Beesmas dreams come true!   
Stick Bug  | You beekeepers are funny with your seasonal traditions. _-Present Given-_ They keep me entertained for sure. Now, let's see what you've lugged up onto this mushroom. ...(krumple krumple)... Oh wow, a xylophone! Those signature playful percussive notes hit you right in the funny bone. I'll make some really funky music with this. Thank you flesh-bug! Now take this: it's a [Partridge In A Stick Bug Ornament]. With that on the tree, you'll receive the following buffs: +25% Convert Rate... +25% Capacity on the Cactus Field and Pepper Patch... And +5% Gifted Bee Pollen. Hope you find it useful. But first, let's dance.   
Honey Bee  | Bzzz zzz zzuh... _-Present Given-_ Bzzz buz ZZ! ZZZZZ BZZ BZZZZ!!! ...(krumple krumple)... (You open up the [Present] for Honey Bee) (The box is filled to the brim with honey) BUZZZZZZZUH BZZZZZZZ! Bzz zz zz! (Honey Bee's eye _[sic]_ open wide with joy) (You can tell it's very grateful) Bzzzz zzz buzz bz BUZ zz. (Honey Bee pulls out a platic _[sic]_ bag full of honey) (Inside floats a crude plastic figurine of Honey Bee) Zzzz! (You can sense that with this on the Beesmas Tree, you'll receive these boosts:) (+25% Convert Rate) (+10% Epic Bee Pollen) (+10% Honey From Tokens) (And +1% Honey Per Goo!) Bzz zuh Buzz buzz BuzzZuhz (Honey Bee eyes your honey meter madly) (You can sense it wants to cash in the IOU immediately)   
Gummy Bear  | A beekeeper approaches, Gummy Bee! _-If the player does not meet the requirements-_ A box wrapped in colorful paper... a gift? You believe you're worthy of exchanging gifts with US? HAH We do not accept trifles from meager meat-based civilians. Prove yourself by completing our quest and we may reconsider. _-If the player meets the requirements-_ What is that in your two hands, Gummy Soldier? A customary delivery... A gesture of GOO-d will. Gummy Bee and I permit you to open it for us. ...(krumple krumple)... OH! You've brought live ammunition. A case of hand GOO-nades! How EXPLOSIVELY sweet. I will put these to good use gumming up a village or two. KABOOM! In exchange, my gooey grunt, I give you this: The [Gummy Globe Ornament]! Let it serve as a constant reminder that better days WILL COME. And the unbearably bitter and salty states of all worlds will give way to a SWEET future. ALL worlds will be GOOnited when Gummy Bee and I are through. And with that ornament on your wooden plant, you'll receive the following boosts to aid in our GOO CRUCADE [sic]. +10% Capacity +25% Coconut Field Capacity +1% Super-Crit Chance And +10% GOO! Now go GOO UP the pathetic Beesmas festivities once and for all!   
Bubble Bee Man  | _Not added yet_  
  
![Hivesticker eviction](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D) | **This piece of content goes bye bye.** The following content has been removed from the game. The contents below may be archival, but feel free to edit below.  
---|---  
  
Presents returned once again in Beesmas 2024 (Winter). They still had their main function of adding [Ornaments](/wiki/Ornaments "Ornaments") to the [Beesmas Tree](/wiki/Beesmas_Tree "Beesmas Tree") when given to NPCs, but could now be given to [Sun Bear](/wiki/Sun_Bear "Sun Bear"). 

## Ways to obtain

  * Multiple quests from Bee Bear awarded a total of ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)10 **Presents** , along with other rewards.
  * [Bee Bear’s Catalog](/wiki/Bee_Bear%E2%80%99s_Catalog "Bee Bear’s Catalog") sold ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 **Present** for ![Snowflake](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)250 [Snowflakes](/wiki/Snowflake "Snowflake") and ![Gingerbread Bear](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)10 [Gingerbread Bears](/wiki/Gingerbread_Bear "Gingerbread Bear").
  * [Onett](/wiki/Onett "Onett") gave ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 **Present** (along with other items) upon claiming a hive for the first time during the event's period.
  * Donating ![Strawberry](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Strawberries](/wiki/Strawberry "Strawberry") to the [Galentine Shrine](/wiki/Wind_Shrine#Galentine_Shrine "Wind Shrine") had a chance to give ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 **Present** , along with other rewards.
  * Completing [Robo Party](/wiki/Robo_Party "Robo Party")'s rank 25 for the first time gave ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 **Present** , along with other rewards.
  * Using the code "LatePresents" gave ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)2 **Presents**.
  * Using the code "LatePresent" gave ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 **Present**.

## Dialogue

NPC  | Dialogue   
---|---  
Black Bear  | Oh, hello there! _-Present Given-_ A present for me? How thoughtful! I wonder what it could be... ...(krumple krumple)... Wow! It's a Vicious Bee themed back scratcher! The prongs look like [Stingers]. Yikes! I've never been a fan of Vicious Bee. It's straight up scary and rude. But my arms are pretty short, so this will really come in handy. Thanks! I've got gifts for you as well! Including the [Honey Jar Ornament]! With this on the Beesmas Tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity in the Sunflower Field and +5% Honey Per Pollen! This should help your honeymaking this Beesmas! Happy Honeydays!   
Mother Bear  | The Honeydays bring the bees so much joy! _-Present Given-_ What's this! Oooh you shouldn't have! Now let's see what you got me. ...(krumple krumple)... Ohhh! It's a little pendant necklace! Just what could be inside? ...(click)... Aww! It's a picture of my cubs having fun at camp! Boy do I miss them. This'll help though! Thank you so much! I've got some presents for you as well, including the [Stuffed Heart Ornament]! With that on the tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity in the Strawberry Field and +10% Pollen from Bee Gathering! Make sure to cherish the time with your bees this Beesmas! That's what it's all about!   
Brown Bear  | Brrrr, it's cold! _-Present Given-_ But not too cold for us to exchange gifts! So what is it, huh? What'd you get ol' Brown Bear? ...(krumple krumple)... Whoa! A 12 month subscription to ChatGPBee! Not sure exactly how I'll use this, but I'll try it out! Maybe it can come up with quests to give you. Or maybe it can just keep me company. Thanks bud! Now look at what I got you, including the [Royal Jelly Ornament]! With that on the Beesmas Tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity in the Clover Field And +20% Pollen from "Bomb" abilities! Happy Beesmas!   
Panda Bear  | Hmm... _-Present Given-_ A gift? Oh, that's kind of you. I'll open it now. ...(krumple krumple)... WHOA! It's 5 [Invigorating Vials]! With these, I can instantly gain Invigorating Nectar to temporarily boost my attack power. Thanks! Well I can't take something without giving you something back. I got you a few gifts, including the [Boxing Glove Ornament]. With that on the tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity in the Bamboo Field And +5% Bee Attack Power! Stay safe out there, and Merry Beesmas.   
Science Bear  | Beesmas is amazing! But it sure keeps us busy. _-Present Given-_ Oh, of course! Time to exchange our gifts. Let’s see what you’ve brought me... Fascinating! It's a 3D Printer! Imagine all the useful tools I'll be able to make with this. Like, hmm... I'll come up with some. Anyways, now for your gift: the [Conical Flask] ornament! With that on the Beesmas Tree, you’ll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity in the Pineapple Patch And +25% Convert Rate at the Hive! That ought to help make this Beesmas productive. Thanks again for all your help. Happy Honeydays!   
Polar Bear  | I'm just happy it's winter. _-Present Given-_ My cousin Bee Bear's entire identity is centered around Beesmas. At first our family thought it was weird, but we've become supportive over the years. Beesmas brings Bee Bear joy and gives him a sense of purpose. Who are we to judge? We all have our quirks. Me, I'm obsessed with cooking. So I hope you brought a cooking related gift! Come on, let me see what you brought. ...(krumple krumple)... Niiiice! An air fryer! I've been wanting one of these for ages. This can crisp up some stuff fast, and'll let me cut back on [Oil]. Now for your gift! The [Cast Iron Skillet] Ornament! With this on the tree, you'll receive these boosts: +25% Convert Rate And +3% Pollen from Colorless Bees! Works for their gathering and abilities! Well, good luck on the rest of your Bee Bear quests. Merry Beesmas!   
Dapper Bear  | You're free to warm yourself up in my shop. _-Present Given-_ Somehow I knew you'd get something for me this year. We're on the same page. I've been thinking about you too. I'll go ahead and open my gift now. ...(krumple krumple)... Ah, cool! An incense stand. I've been wanting to spruce up the aromatics of my shop. The sweet, woody notes of incense might be a perfect fit. Thank you! Now, I just so happened to get you something fragrant as well: the [Potpourri Ornament]. It's filled with flowers and leaves I grew in my Planters. Smells like Beesmas! With this on the tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity in the Dandelion Field And +5% Nectar. Hope you like it. Happy Honeydays!   
Onett  | Is it snowing? I can't tell from in this... cave.. _-Present Given-_ A [Present] for me? I'll try to act surprised! Oh boy, here I go opening it... ...(krumple krumple)... Nice! It's the bottle of sublingual melatonin tablets that I totally didn't order for myself! You know, in some parts of the world this stuff is difficult to get. RX, even. Crazy, right? Cause I can't sleep without it. Anyways, thanks! And here's my gift to you: Bringing Beesmas back this December when I said I wouldn't, and actually having the holiday-themed update overlap with Christmas. ... Just kidding! It's the [Candy Cane Reindeer Ornament]! With this on the Beesmas tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity on the Mountain Top Field and +20% Tool Pollen! Hope your Honeydays are full of fun and quality time with those you love. Merry Beesmas!   
Spirit Bear  | Mmmm...mhmm...(she's murmuring to herself with her eyes close [sic])... _-Present Given-_ I've been counting [Snowflakes] all day as they fall onto the fields. (Yawn). Oh, it's quite hypnotic! I keep losing count because, well, it's like counting sheep. At one point, though, I counted. What was it... Over 50 [Snowflakes]? I lost count that time because of a Snow Storm. I mean, I can't keep up with that. ... Snow everywhere, like frosting on our Beesmas cake! Decorated with all those delightful contraptions this year. Like that wreath. Hehehh, Black Bear, bless his heart. Look at that Honey Wreath. I won't say more... Oh but Mother Bear's Gingerbread House! Oh, I like that one. ...(she's staring into the distance)... Is that a [Snowflake]? Or... Hmm. I'm tired of counting. I'm bored now. Why bother with something so mundane, in the most exciting time of the year! Bee Bear, oh that precious fool. Don't tell him I called him a fool. Not that he would mind. To think that we mere mortal bears could offend a honeyday deity. Anyways, that sweet, jovial bear, he brightens up the entire mountain in the darkest days of the year. How did we even make it through the winters before him? Well, I mean, I do remember... It wasn't so bad. We just celebrated, we just didn't focus so much on the bees. Not that you bees don't deserve it! Bee Bear is completely right about that. We celebrated the passing of the year in a more general sense. We still ate a lot of the same food, did similar customs, it just felt a little more... off the cusp. We still put ornaments on a tree, you see, we just didn't have a special name for it. Or did we? Hmmm... Was it... Oh, like I'll ever remember. Mother always had a tree, and we dolled it up for winter. Left it up much longer than we probably should have, we were never... punctual. But it was always so, so full of ornaments! Everyone was jealous of our tree. Perhaps the only time our hoarding tendencies were a source of envy. Hah! And things really took off as Beesmas became more and more of a thing. Mother couldn't help herself, any time she saw an ornament on sale. Mmhmm. And some of those - completely unnecessary, Mother. The only thing more imposing than our tree was the size of our debt. How did we even get approved for so many credit cards? Yaach... But it was worth it. You can't put a price on Beesmas Magic. Well, you can, sort of. Just take a look on Bee Bear's Catalog. But you understand what I mean. Those debts are long gone, and here I am countless years later and still reminiscing about our trees. Mother's taste for ornaments - they were well considered. ...(she's staring off into the distance)... ...31... Or was it? Drats. I've lost count again. I can't help myself! I just see the [Snowflakes] and I just start... But it's too hard! And it's boring. Let's do something else. What have you come to see me about, anyhow, dear? What's that you're holding - it's for me? Oh! A gift! Will you open it for Spirit Bear? My hands are full, you know. And sore. ...(You open the present for Spirit Bear)... ...(krumple krumple)... Oh no! Oh no no no! This - this is just too much! It's a Windy Bee Bobble Head! So silly! Looks just like you, doesn't it? ... ...(shes [sic] staring off in the distance)... Yes it does. OK, goodbye now. ... Oh! YOUR gift! Yes yes I have one for you! A few things, actually. Including my [Memory Match Ornament]! With that on the tree, well, I don't quite recall what it does. You can check the tree yourself. (Yawn). First, why not check out that strange [Snowflake] in the Dandelion Field. Merry Beesmas, dear!   
Robo Bear  | (click click click click click) _-Present Given-_ CHARITABLE GIFT DETECTED. I did not foresee this event occurring! >>Commencing Unwrapping Sequence ...(krumple krumple)... I have received: USB C to USB C Cable, Type C 60W (3.1A) Fast Charging Cable (20FT, 2Pack) Compatible with beePhone 16+16 Pro / beePad Mini 6 / beeBook Pro / Beesung S24+S24 Ultra / + More (USB 2.0, Green). I will be able to utilize these. I have many compatible USB C ports. Thank You! [sic] In return, I will grant you this: The [Digital Pet Ornament]! With that on the Beesmas Tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +10% Capacity +25% Capacity in the Spider Field +10% Pollen from Event Bees and +5% Pollen from Duped Abilities! >> GIFT EXCHANGE SEQUENCE COMPLETE: OUTCOME SUCCESS   
Gifted Riley Bee  | I should have built a roof like Bucko Bee... _-Present Given-_ NUH-UH! You didn’t! You DID! Let me open it, let me open it! ...(krumple krumple)... Alright! Some rechargeable wing warmers!!! Between these and the candles, I MIGHT be able to make it through this stupid, cold, long winter. Just barely. Thank you!! Now here's your gift: the [Electric Candle Ornament]! With that on the Beesmas Tree, you’ll receive these boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity in the Rose and Mushroom Field and +15% Pollen from Flames! Merry Beesmas! Now if you’ll excuse me, I’m gonna go warm up my flappers.   
Gifted Bucko Bee  | Looks chilly out there! _-Present Given-_ Wait, don't tell me you've brought a [Present]? Of all the beekeepers I've met, you really are the coolest! One second, let me just try to unwrap this with my wings somehow... ...(krumple krumple)... What's this? Oooh, it's a jigsaw puzzle! Looks like it's supposed to be a picture of the ocean against a blue sky. Man, that's a lot of blue... might be tricky. I'll try my best! Thanks! And now some gifts for you, including the [Snowman Ornament]! With that on the Beesmas Tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity in the Blue Flower Field and Pine Tree Forest and +15% Pollen from Bubbles! Hope that helps! Merry blue Beesmas!   
Stick Bug  | You beekeepers are funny with your seasonal traditions. _-Present Given-_ I won't judge though. We all move to our own beat. So, let's see what you've brought me. ...(krumple krumple)... It's a pair of boutique double sided headphones! You know, the major players in the headphones space have been really slacking. Innovative new start-ups run by passionate audiophiles are on the up and up! These have been getting awesome reviews. I'll put them to good use. Now for your gift: it's the [Partridge in a Stick Bug Ornament]. With that on the tree, you'll receive the following buffs: +25% Convert Rate... +25% Capacity on the Cactus Field and Pepper Patch... And +5% Gifted Bee Pollen. But that'll have to wait.   
Sun Bear  | I'm just chilling. _-Present Given-_ Really cool of you to think of giving a [Present] to a tourist like me! Guess as long as I'm here, I should participate in the festivities. Well, what'd you get me? ...(krumple krumple)... Haha! It's a Bee Swarm Simulator bumper sticker! This'll go great on the back of my RV. Another fine piece of my traveling memorabilia. And hey, I'm no jerk. Of course I got you something too: It's the [Steering Wheel Ornament]! With that on the Beesmas Tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate +5% Pollen from Legendary Bees And +5% Ability Token Lifespan! That thing's one of a kind. Consider yourself lucky. I'm glad I stuck around! Merry Beesmas, as they say around here!

![Hivesticker eviction](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D) | **This piece of content goes bye bye.** The following content has been removed from the game. The contents below may be archival, but feel free to edit below.  
---|---  
  
Presents returned once again in Beesmas 2024 (Summer). They still had their main function of adding [Ornaments](/wiki/Ornaments "Ornaments") to the [Beesmas Tree](/wiki/Beesmas_Tree "Beesmas Tree") when given to NPCs. 

## Ways to obtain

  * Multiple quests from Bee Bear awarded a total of ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)12 **Presents** , along with other rewards.
  * [Bee Bear’s Catalog](/wiki/Bee_Bear%E2%80%99s_Catalog "Bee Bear’s Catalog") sold ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 **Present** for ![Snowflake](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)250 [Snowflakes](/wiki/Snowflake "Snowflake") and ![Gingerbread Bear](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)10 [Gingerbread Bears](/wiki/Gingerbread_Bear "Gingerbread Bear").
  * [Onett](/wiki/Onett "Onett") gave ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 **Present** (along with other items) upon claiming a hive for the first time during the event's period.
  * Donating ![Strawberry](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Strawberries](/wiki/Strawberry "Strawberry") to the [Galentine Shrine](/wiki/Wind_Shrine#Galentine_Shrine "Wind Shrine") had a chance to give ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 **Present** , along with other rewards.
  * Completing [Robo Party](/wiki/Robo_Party "Robo Party")'s rank 25 for the first time gave ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 **Present** , along with other rewards.

## Dialogue

NPC  | Dialogue   
---|---  
Black Bear  | Oh, hello there! _-Present Given-_ You're the first person to bring me a [Present] this year Which is strange, because I talk to basically every beekeeper. I can't wait to see what you got me! ...(krumple krumple)... Wow! A honey-scented bowling ball! No wonder this was sooo heavy. I didn't even know they made scented bowling balls. I've also never gone bowling. But now I'm going to give it a shot! Thanks a bunch! I got something for you as well: the [Honey Jar Ornament]! With this on the Beesmas Tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate, +25% Capacity in the Sunflower Field and +5% Honey Per Pollen! That should help your honeymaking this Beesmas!   
Mother Bear  | The Honeydays bring the bees so much joy! _\--Present Given-_ Oh! Is this for me? How very sweet of you! I'll open it right now! ...(krumple krumple)... Aww! It's a trendy tumbler that can keep my drinks cool! Or warm, considering it's winter... Or is it winter? I'm so confused! My cubs are away at summer camp, yet this place is covered in snow. That's ok though. I'm just greatful _[sic]_ to have you around to share the Honeydays with. Now for your gifts! I got you a few things A [Gingerbread Bear], some [Gumdrops] and [Moon Charms]... And the [Stuffed Heart Ornament]! With this on the tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate, +25% Capacity in the Strawberry Field and +10% Pollen from Bees! Merry Beesmas! Hope you enjoy those! I'm off to fill my tumbler with a strawberry spritz.   
Brown Bear  | Man, it's REALLY cold this summer! _-Present Given-_ You got something in that [Present] that could warm me up? ...(krumple krumple)... Whoa! It's a 90 day membership to Beequinox, the bougiest bee-themed gym around! I think they've even got a steam room! That'll warm me up for sure. Is this you hinting that I need to work out more? Haha! Just playing. Thanks bud! Now check out what I got YOU: the [Royal Jelly Ornament]! With that on the Beesmas Tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate, +25% Capacity in the Clover Field And +20% Pollen from "Bomb" abilities! Happy Beesmas!   
Panda Bear  | Hmm... _-Present Given-_ A gift? Well, thank you. I'll open it now. ...(krumple krumple)... WHOA! A [Rose Headband Beequip] with a +9% Bee Attack Hive Bonus! I didn't even know they got that high. I don't have any bees that can equip this, but I'll lend this out to beekeepers in need. I got you something as well. It's the [Boxing Glove Ornament]. With that on the tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate; +25% Capacity in the Bamboo Field; And +5% Bee Attack Power! Stay safe out there, and Merry Beesmas.   
Science Bear  | Beesmas in July? How peculiar... _-Present Given-_ Time has been operating rather strangely this year. Somehow the seasons have warped! Could the passage of time itself be altering? Time-dilation? Climate change? What gives? Either way, we still need to exchange our Beesmas [Presents]. I'll open mine first! ...(krumple krumple)... Fascinating! An intricate poster displaying every bee type discovered so far! Oh, but on closer inspection, some of these don't look right... In fact, some are missing wings, and others are in strange, organic, non-cube shapes! Surely this poster was generated by AI. How interesting! I'd be curious to know the prompt you used. Thank you! Now, for you, I've got something very special: the [Conical Flask] ornament! With that on the Beesmas Tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate, +25% Capacity in the Pineapple Patch and +25% Convert Rate at the Hive! That should speed up the conversion process considerably. However, it won't apply to conversion that occurs in the flower fields. Thanks again for all your help. Happy Honeydays! Now if you'll excuse me, I'm going to investigate this Summer Beesmas phenomenon further...   
Polar Bear  | I was worried it wasn't going to snow at all this year... _-Present Given-_ Bee Bear is my cousin, and he's been eccentric from the start. Something's been up with him, and I wonder if that's why Beesmas is so late. Or, early? I don't even know anymore. He operates by his own rules. And apparently he also rules the weather! He's sure got some crazy technology up his sleeves. Anyways, let's see what you brought me! ...(krumple krumple)... Niiiice! It's a bottle of Truffle Oil! This will really spruce up my dishes with an earthy, umami flavor! How many Puffshrooms did you have to pop to get this? Thank you! Now for your present: the [Cast Iron Skillet Ornament]! With this on the tree, you'll receive these boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity in the Pumpkin Patch And +10% White Field Capacity! Thanks for humoring my cousin, and good luck with all his Sticker quests! Happy Honeydays!   
Dapper Bear  | You're free to warm yourself up in my shop. _-Present Given-_ A present? You must really like me, huh? Well, the feeling's mutual! Let's open these bad boys up. ...(krumple krumple)... Of course! A [Sticker Planter]! So up my alley. These colorful, single-use planters are guaranteed to give Stickers when they're harvested. I'm assuming you got this from the Sticker Sprout in the Hub Field, right? So neat. You know, when I first moved here, Stickers weren't a thing. But now that they're here, I'm obsessed. They're perfect little tools of self-expression. Quirky, varied, and extremely collectable. I'd love to integrate them into my identity somehow. This [Sticker Planter] will kick-start my journey. Thanks, genuinely! And now for you, heh, a [Ticket Planter], a Nectar Vial... And the [Potpourri Ornament]. It's filled with flowers and leaves I grew in my Planters. The fragrance is pretty strong... but hey, smells like Beesmas. With this on the tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity in the Dandelion Field And +5% Nectar. Hope you like it. Thanks for kickstarting my Sticker collection! And good luck with yours. Happy Honeydays!   
Onett  | Is it snowing? I can't tell from in this... cave.. _-Present Given-_ A [Present] for me? Wow, thanks! Ok, let's see what it is. ...(krumple krumple)... Oh! It's a calandar.. _[sic]_ I get it, cause I'm releasing Beesmas in July. Hah, that's funny! Well, I appreciate it, but this probably won't help me. I'll either forget to use it, or I'll lose it... I lose everything. It's a real problem. But thanks anyways! I got something for you too. It's the [Candy Cane Reindeer Ornament]! I made it myself. With this on the Beesmas tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate, +25% Capacity on the Mountain Top Field and +20% Tool Pollen! Well, hope you enjoy it and have fun honeymaking on your summer break (if you're on it)! Remember to use the Sticker Stack - the boost adds up. Merry Beesmas!   
Spirit Bear  | Mmmm...mhmm...(she's murmuring to herself with her eyes close [sic])... _-Present Given-_ ... Huh? I'm up, I'm up. Hello. Good to you see you. [sic] Hmm... what? Wait. What's in your hands? Oh! What? Wait a second... (She looks around) Why have you got that? Why does everything look like this? What's going on? What day IS it, dear??? ... That doesn't add up. But I've honestly no sense of time any more. [sic] You could tell me it was October and I'd believe you. Although, I had a suspicion I had forgotten something this year... Hmm. Oh! Wait. (She reaches into her [Petal Bag]) Heheh, I hadn't forgotten at all! I've had a [Present] waiting for you in all along... How long has it been in there? So long I don't even remember what it is! But it has your name on it, so surely it's meant for you. The one you brought me looks nicer, though. So I suppose I should go ahead and open it. Unless... ... Would it not be wrong? To open this now... willy nilly... whenever... I thought the point of Beesmas gifts was that they were an annual tradition? The rhythms of the seasons, accentuated by the Honeydays, or other customs... Produces a familiar sway that acts as a point of reference, no? For our progress... For our growth... Mmmh. They give the structure to our lives upon which change can grow! Our daily, our monthly, our yearly routines establish themselves like vines on that lattice. So would we dare shake it up!? (Cough) Dare to exchange these NOW? And risk losing that rhythm? Might knock our petals off! Before you know it, you'll be just like me. Lost in a timeless, sleepy fog. No clue what month it is. ... Hmm, but you do have a point, dear. The rhythm's already lost! Perhaps we've already moved on to this, mmm, post-annular era. Perhaps the years themselves are getting longer... Or maybe tradition was alawys a silly concept. A cage more than a stage. I mean, I've always been an innovator. A trailblaizer. [sic] Always tried to switch things up - to never settle into a groove. So why shouldn't Beesmas be now? We could open these [Presents] up, and begin to make it so. So. Let's see what you brought Spirit Bear! ...(krumple krumple)... Oh! A Flying Windy Bee Sticker! I don't remember seeing this sticker in the, what do you call it That menu that lists all the Stickers, you know, dear. The compendium? No. That's not right. ... The Index! Yes, of course. The Sticker Index. Well, last I checked this Sticker wasn't in it. That makes it extra special! Thank you so much, dear! I'll stick this somewhere nice. (She sticks it on her [Petal Wand]) Ok. Now, you open yours! Go on! ...(krumple krumple)... That's the [Memory Match Ornament]! My Memory Match Ornament. The one I won myself way back in... well, it was a long time ago. Oh, it says right there: way back in 1983! Probably before you were born, you do look so young. With that on the tree, you'll receive these boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity in the Stump Field And +5% from Bee Abilities! Don't think they give these out anymore. The Memory Match Committee isn't what it used to be. People don't need to practice to keep their memory sharp when they've got smartphones. Every answer in their [sic] As if Memory Match actually helps with memory at all. I mean, look at me. Memory Match Champ and I can't even keep track of the month. But you enjoy that, it'll look nice on the tree! Happy Honeydays, I suppose!

![Hivesticker eviction](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D) | **This piece of content goes bye bye.** The following content has been removed from the game. The contents below may be archival, but feel free to edit below.  
---|---  
  
Presents returned once again in Beesmas 2022. They still had their main function of adding [Ornaments](/wiki/Ornaments "Ornaments") to the [Beesmas Tree](/wiki/Beesmas_Tree "Beesmas Tree") when given to NPCs, but could now be given to the new NPC, [Robo Bear](/wiki/Robo_Bear "Robo Bear"). 

## Ways to obtain

  * Multiple quests from Bee Bear awarded a total of ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)12 **Presents** , along with other rewards.
  * [Bee Bear’s Catalog](/wiki/Bee_Bear%E2%80%99s_Catalog "Bee Bear’s Catalog") sold ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 **Present** for ![Snowflake](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)250 [Snowflakes](/wiki/Snowflake "Snowflake") and ![Gingerbread Bear](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)10 [Gingerbread Bears](/wiki/Gingerbread_Bear "Gingerbread Bear").
  * [Onett](/wiki/Onett "Onett") gave ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 **Present** (along with other items) upon claiming a hive for the first time during the event's period.
  * Donating ![Strawberry](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Strawberries](/wiki/Strawberry "Strawberry") to the [Galentine Shrine](/wiki/Wind_Shrine#Galentine_Shrine "Wind Shrine") had a chance to give ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 **Present** , along with other rewards.
  * Completing [Robo Party](/wiki/Robo_Party "Robo Party")'s rank 25 for the first time gave ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 **Present** , along with other rewards.

## Dialogue

NPC  | Dialogue   
---|---  
Black Bear  | Oh, hello there! _-Present Given-_ What've you gotten me this year? I'll bet it's something special ...(krumple krumple)... Wow! 12 Honey-glazed donuts, with Beesmas sprinkles!! You know, they don't use the best honey on these things. Companies are always trying to cut cost. But I'm not complaining. I'll savor these for sure! Thank you so much! Now here's your present: it's the [Honey Jar Ornament]! With this on the Beesmas Tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate, +25% Capacity in the Sunflower Field and +5% Honey Per Pollen! Enjoy! And Happy Honeydays!   
Mother Bear  | The honeydays bring the bees so much joy! _-Present Given-_ Oh! What's this? A present for me? You're the sweetest! ...(krumple krumple)... Oooh! An adorable Puppy Bee Pillow! The Puppy Bee and I share a special connection: we're both about bond with bees. That was very thoughtful of you! No [Reindeer Antlers] on it though. Maybe next year? *wink wink* Hehe. Well, I've also got some gifts for you: A [Gingerbread Bear}, _[sic]_ some [Glitter]... And the [Stuffed Heart Ornament]! With this on the tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate, +25% Capacity in the Strawberry Field and +10% Pollen from Bees! Can't wait to test my new pillow out tonight. Merry Beesmas!   
Brown Bear  | Man, it's cold! _-Present Given-_ Maybe your present will help warm me up! Can't wait to find out. ...(krumple krumple)... Whoa! A $25 Ubear Eats gift card! I'm ordering some warm soup and hot cocoa right away. I hope they deliver to ROBLOX games... Thanks bud. Now check out what I got you, some [Royal Jelly], A [Glue]... And the [Royal Jelly Ornament]! With that on the Beesmas Tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate, +25% Capacity in the Clover Field and +20% Pollen from "bomb" abilities! Happy Beesmas!   
Panda Bear  | Hmm... _-Present Given-_ I can't help but feel like there's something wrong this Beesmas. Something... robotic and menacing. Let's exchange presents quickly. We might not have much time. ...(krumple krumple)... Nice! A bamboo baseball bat! This could help if things get sketchy. If that robot over there gets up to no good, I'll give it a few good WHACKS. Now you take this: the [Boxing Glove Ornament]. I got you a few gifts, including the [Boxing Glove Ornament]. With that on the tree, you'll recieve the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity in the Bamboo Field And +5% Bee Attack Power! Stay alert! I don't trust that Robo Bear...   
Science Bear  | Beesmas is amazing! But it sure keeps us busy. _-Present Given-_ This is a good time to go ahead and exchange our gifts, though. Let's see what you brought me... ...(krumple krumple... Fascinating! A very peculiar sort of green [Cog]. You found it on the flowers you say? Hmm... Could this be related to the Robo Bear who recently appeared on the mountain? I'll have to investigate more. For now take these gifts: A [White Balloon], an [Atomic Treat]... And the [Conical Flask] ornament! With that on the Beesmas Tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate, +25% Capacity in the Pineapple Patch and +25% Convert Rate at the Hive! That ought to help make your beesmas productive. Now if you'll excuse me, I'm going to go run some tests.   
Polar Bear  | I'm just happy it's Winter. _-Present Given-_ My cousin Bee Bear's been all about Beesmas for as long as I can remember. I used to worry about his obsession. But I've grown to accept it. At the end of the day, he brings joy to tons of beekeepers. I aim to do the same thing through my cooking. We aren't so different. Anyways, let's see what you brought me! ...(krumple krumple)... Oooh yeah! A cog-operated Blender! Finally, a blender that's not taller than I am. The one in the Badge Bearer's Guild is ridiculous. Do you know how hard it's been to make soups in _[sic]_ smoothies in that thing? How many hours I've spent wiping that thing down? Well no more! This gift will change my life. Now for your present! Some [Oil], [Gingerbread Bears]... And the [Cast Iron Skillet] Ornament! With this on the tree, you'll receive the these boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity in the Pumpkin Patch And +10% White Field Capacity! Well, good luck on the rest of your Bee Bear quests. Happy honeydays!   
Dapper Bear  | You're free to warm yourself up in my shop. _-Present Given-_ Somehow I knew you'd get something for me this year. We're on the same page. I've been thinking about you too. I'll go ahead and open my gift now. ...(krumple krumple)... Ah hah, a funky little Bumble Bee pin for my lapel! I don't usually like accessories as gifts - I prefer to pick my own. But this one hits the right spot. Thank you! Now why don't you open mine? There's a few things in there: A [Ticket Planter], a vial of [Motivating Nectar]... _[sic]_ And the [Potpourri Ornament]. It's filled with flowers and leaves I grew in my Planters. The fragrance is pretty strong... but hey, smells like Beesmas. With this on the tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate, +25% Capacity in the Dandelion Field, And +5% Nectar. Hope you like it. I know I'll be flaunting this pin around for weeks. Thanks again, and Happy Honeydays.   
Onett  | 3 months of Beesmas! Yipee. _-Present Given-_ A [Present]! What a surprise! I bet you're giving this to me because you've heard I give the best gifts back. That doesn't mean I don't appreciate it! Of course I do. Come on. Uhh... let's just continue. ...(krumple. krumple)... Wow!! A finger painting of Basic Bee! And there's a Rascal Bee! They look like they're fighting a... is that an Armored Aphid? OH! A Cogmower! Of course. This is going straight on my refridgerator. My gift's handmade too, but not quite as impressive. It's the [Candy Cane Reindeer Ornament]! +25% Convert Rate, +25% Capacity on the Mountain Top Field and +20% Tool Pollen! I also got you an assortment of beans. No [Festive Bean] though... Anyways, good luck with Robo Bear. Thanks for the painting. Merry Beesmas!   
Spirit Bear  | Mmmmmhm... Lots of robots around all of a sudden Making a lot of noise. ... Whenever they walk around, I swear Sounds like an elephant falling down escalator. Not a very considerate sound, hm? ... And my ears aren't even that good Mmmhm...No good. I've never been a huge fan of machines. I don't like how they move so sharply. So abruptly, yet so precisely. There's no flow to it. It's a type of cold, calculated movement that could break your bones Without even realizing it! Imagine getting your fingers pinched in one of those cogs. Oooh, sends a shiver down my spine. And the noises! Achk. Just as sharp! Just as sudden! The types of noises that, sure, may be found in nature But only on rare occasions. Usually dangerous ones. Earthquakes. Thunderstorms. The culminated cries of accumulated forces. Not birdsong! Not background noise! ... I'm whining. I'll stop. Anyways, what brings you up here? What's that behind your back? Is it what I think it is? A [Present] for me! I was beginning to think that you forgot. Silly me! Will you open it for spirit bear? ...(krumple krumple)... Ooooo! What I it? ...A bracelet? No, no. Oooh, I see! A sleeping eye mask, shaped like a cloud! How precious! Oh, and so soft! As if I didn't sleep enough already, heheh. Thank you, dear. very thoughtful of you. I'll test it out as soon as you take off. ... Oh, but I can't leave you empty handed. I prepared a small basket of gifts for you Including the [Memory Match Ornament]! The one I won myself in a Memory Match tournament back in... Some time long ago, I don't remember exactly. It says on the ornament. With that on the tree you will receive the following boosts: +25% Convert rate +25% Capacity in the Stump Field And +500% Pollen! ... No, that can't be right. Sorry. Let me check. ... Ah. It's +5% Pollen from Bee Abilities. Still useful. (Yawn) Enjoy! Now if you'll excuse me... I'd like to try this mask out.   
Robo Bear  | (click click click click click) _-Present Given-_ CHARITABLE GIFT DETECTED. I did not foresee this event occurring! >>Commencing Unwrapping Sequence ...(krumple krumple)... It appears I've been gifted a [Yellow Drive]! These are unfortunately incompatible with my motherboard. But I acknowledge and appreciate this gesture of camaraderie. In return I will grant you this: The [Digital Pet Ornament]! With that on the Beesmas Tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate, +25% Capacity in Spider Field, +10% Pollen from Event Abilities and +5% Pollen from Duped Abilities! Thank you generous beekeeper. Enjoy your April!   
Gifted Riley Bee  | Bucko Bee better not use that Snow Machine! It's TOO DANG COLD! -_Present Given-_ WHAT! A GIFT? No way! Youuuuuuuu Well hand it over! Come on! ...(krumple krumple)... Oh... my... gumdrops... A permanent Precise Mark! How did you get this?? Did it just, like, fail to despawn? Man, I know some beekeepers who would DIE for one of these. This is going to raise the Red HQ straight to the top! I got you something too. Not quite as OP though. It's the [Electric Candle Ornament]! With that on the Beesmas Tree, you'll recieve these boosts: +25% Convert Rate, +25% Capacity in the Rose Field and Mushroom Field, and +10% Pollen from Flames! Now let's get out there and heat up the Honeydays! RED ROCKS!   
Gifted Bucko Bee  | I'm just happy it's Winter. _-Present Given-_ Well, if it isn't the coolest beekeeper around! You bringing me a [Present] makes you even cooler. Let's see what it is... ...(krumple krumple)... Well I'll be! An entire gallon of Comforting Nectar! No wonder that thing was so heavy. Soo... I happened to get you some Comforting Nectar as well.. That's just part of my gift though, so this isn't too awkward. The most important part is the [Snowman Ornament]! With that on the Beesmas Tree, you'll recieve the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate, +25% Capacity in the Blue Flower Field and Pine Tree Forest, and +10% Blue Field Capacity! Thanks for the Nectar. Have a merry blue Beesmas!   
Stick Bug  | You flesh bugs are funny with your Beesmas traditions. _-Present Given-_ Fun to watch though. The nymphs get a kick out of it as well. Ok, let's see what you've brought me. ...(krumple krumple)... Ah hah, clever! A [Robo Pass]! I guess it would be pretty cool to see how my nymphs and I fair _[sic]_ against those wacky robots. But I unfortunately can't accept. The Robo Bear and I have an arrangement. Can't attack each other. Only you lucky beekeepers. It's the thought that counts, though. Thanks flesh bug. You keep that [Robo Pass], and take this as well: The [Partridge In A Stick Bug Ornament]. With that on the tree, you'll receieve the following buffs: +25% Convert Rate, +25% Capacity on the Cactus Field and Pepper Patch, and +5% Pollen. Put that up on the tree quick so we can have some fun.   
Honey Bee  | Bzz zzz zzuh... _-Present Given-_ Bzzz buz ZZ! ZZZZZ BZZ BZZZZ!!! ...(krumple krumple)... (You open up the [Present] for Honey Bee) (The box is filled to the brim with honey) BUZZZZZZZUH BZZZZZZZ! Bzz zz zz! (Honey Bee's eye open wide with joy) (You can tell it's very grateful) Bzzzz zzz buzz bz BUZ zz. (Honey Bee pulls out a plastic bag full of honey) (Inside floats a crude plastic figurine of Honey Bee) Zzzz! HAH We do not accept trifles from meager meat-based civilians. Prove yourself by completing our quest and we may reconsider. _-If the player has completed his quest-_ All seasonal customs of you beekeepers will soon be replaced. A sweet, gooey-culture will envelope ALL seasons in time! Beesmas? NOPE! Halloween? DENIED! President's day? President's NO WAY! HAH! Every day will be Gummy Day. And all will know eternal sweetness. But I will accept your [Present], Gummy Soldier. For now... ...(krumple krumple)... Yes... YES! A 3lb Gummy Worm. This is satisfactory. Gummy Bee and I shall absorb it with haste to enhance our powers. Or perhaps I'll launch it out my Gummy Cannon! RAIN A WRATH OF WORMS UPON MY FOES! Yes, many delicious options. Now take this: The [Gummy Globe Ornament]! With that ornament on the pathetic tree of Beesmas, you'll recieve the following boosts: +10% Capacity, +25% Coconut Field Capacity, +1% Super-Crit Chance, and +10% GOO! USE IT WELL!

Oth4er than the bees It's complicated but...... Basically it's a waste to give this to me. Right? Give it to someone real. who actually has a body. and can use it. ![Hivesticker eviction](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D) | **This piece of content goes bye bye.** The following content has been removed from the game. The contents below may be archival, but feel free to edit below.  
---|---  
  
Presents returned in Beesmas 2021. They functioned the same as the years before that they have appeared in, but could now be given to the new NPC, [Dapper Bear](/wiki/Dapper_Bear "Dapper Bear"). Presents were the only way to obtain [Ornaments](/wiki/Ornaments "Ornaments") from NPCs, and they were needed to be delivered to do quests from [Bee Bear](/wiki/Bee_Bear "Bee Bear"). 

## Ways to obtain

  * Multiple quests from Bee Bear awarded a total of ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)12 **Presents** , along with other rewards.
  * [Bee Bear’s Catalog](/wiki/Bee_Bear%E2%80%99s_Catalog "Bee Bear’s Catalog") sold ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 **Present** for ![Snowflake](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)250 [Snowflakes](/wiki/Snowflake "Snowflake") and ![Gingerbread Bear](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)10 [Gingerbread Bears](/wiki/Gingerbread_Bear "Gingerbread Bear").
  * [Onett](/wiki/Onett "Onett") gave ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 **Present** (along with other items) upon claiming a hive for the first time during the event's period.
  * Donating ![Strawberry](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Strawberries](/wiki/Strawberry "Strawberry") to the [Galentine Shrine](/wiki/Wind_Shrine#Galentine_Shrine "Wind Shrine") had a chance to give ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 **Present** , along with other rewards.

## Dialogue

NPC  | Dialogue   
---|---  
Black Bear  | Oh, hello there! _-Present Given-_ A present for me? Well thank you! I can't wait to find out what it is... ...(krumple krumple)... Oh, it's a honey decanter! Most people don't know this, but letting your honey air out a bit improves the taste. I'm preaching to the choir though. Because you have good taste too! You know me so well. Now let me give you some gifts! Including the [Honey Jar Ornament]! With this on the Beesmas Tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity in the Sunflower Field and +5% Honey Per Pollen! Hope you like it! Happy Honeydays!   
Mother Bear  | The Honeydays bring the bees so much joy! _-Present Given-_ Oh! For me? You shouldn’t have! Let’s see what it is....(krumple krumple)...Oh my gosh, a precious Cub Buddy salt shaker set! Look, Black Cub for Pepper and Polar Cub for salt. I love it! They remind me of my own cubs. Thank you so much! Now for your gifts! A [Gingerbread Bear], a jar of [Oil]...And the [Stuffed Heart Ornament]! With this on the tree, you’ll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity in the Strawberry Field And +10% Pollen from Bees! Now of you’ll excuse me, I’m going to go fill these up!   
Brown Bear  | Man, it's cold! _-Present Given-_ But not too cold for us to exchange gifts! So what is it, huh? What'd you get ol' Brown Bear? Whoa! A 1 year subscription to Bearmazon Prime! I'll get so much out of this, you have NO idea! Thanks bud. Now look at what I got you, including the [Royal Jelly Ornament]! With that on the Beesmas Tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate; +25% Capacity in the Clover Field; And +20% Pollen from "Bomb" abilities! Happy Beesmas!   
Panda Bear  | Hmm... _-Present Given-_ A gift? Oh, that's kind of you. I'll open it now. ...(krumple krumple)... WHOA! A bamboo pull-up bar! This'll help me work on my back muscles. Well, I can't take something without giving you something too. I got you a few gifts, including the [Boxing Glove Ornament]. With that on the tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate; +25% Capacity in the Bamboo Field; And +5% Bee Attack Power! Stay safe out there, and Merry Beesmas.   
Science Bear  | Beesmas is amazing! But it sure keeps us busy. _-Present Given-_ Oh, of course! Time to exchange our gifts. Let’s see what you’ve brought me...Fascinating! A BI-84 scientific calculator! These reliable devices are the standard for universities. Despite being unchanged for over 15 years, they still sell for their original price! Anyways, now for your gifts! Some [Gumdrops], [Neonberries]...And the [Conical Flask] ornament! With that on the Beesmas Tree, you’ll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity in the Pineapple Patch And +25% Convert Rate at the Hive! That ought to help make this Beesmas productive. Thanks again for all your help. Happy Honeydays!   
Polar Bear  | I'm just happy it's Winter. _-Present Given-_ My cousin Bee Bear's entire identity is centered around Beesmas. At first our family thought it was weird, but we've become supportive over the years. Beesmas brings Bee Bear joy and gives him a sense of purpose. Who are we to judge? We all have our quirks. Me, I'm obsessed with cooking. So I hope you brought a cooking related gift! Come on, let me see what you brought. ...(krumple krumple)... Niiiice! A pressure cooker! I've been wanting one of these for ages. For some dishes, this can cut cooking times in half. Now for your gift Some [Enzymes], [Gingerbread Bears]... And the [Cast Iron Skillet] Ornament! With this on the tree, you'll receive these boosts: +25% Convert Rate, +25% Capacity in the Pumpkin Patch, and +10% White Field Capacity! Well, good luck on the rest of your Bee Bear quests. Happy honeydays!   
Dapper Bear  | You're free to warm yourself up in my shop. _-Present Given-_ A gift for me? But we hardly know each other. That must mean whatever's in this box is based off first impressions. Well, let's see how you've sized me up. ...(krumple krumple)... Ah! So you think I look smart, huh? It's a book: a Brief History of The Bee Swarm Simulator Mountain. Guess you'll be relieved to hear that I do in fact dabble in books, And I often turn to history as a primary source for inspiration. Thanks, cool stranger. Would you think it weird if I had some gifts waiting for you too? If you did - I wouldn't mind. I got you some [Snowflakes], a [Ticket Planter], A [Warm Scarf], and something I made myself: The [Potpourri Ornament]. It's filled with flowers and leaves I grew in my Planters. The fragrance is pretty strong... but hey, smells like Beesmas. With this on the tree, you'll receive the following boosts: +25% Convert Rate, +25% Capacity in the Dandelion Field, And +5% Nectar. You're not the only one who judges a book by it's cover. That's why I think we'll be good friends soon enough.   
Onett  | Is it snowing? I can't tell from in this... cave.. _-Present Given-_ What's that? Uh oh! You didn't have to get me anything. Increasing the player count is a gift in itself. But I guess it would be rude not to open it, right? Ok well here I go! ...(krumple krumple)... Wow!! It's a humidifier! It does get really dry in here during the winter. When it gets dry out, my skin starts acting up - gets really dry. And my contacts start to irritate my eyes. So this is perfect! I hope its powerful to fill in this entire den, or whatever it is were standing in. Anyways, I can't thank you enough. But I can give you this: The [Candy Cane Reindeer Ornament]! With it on the Beesmas Tree, you'll recieve these boosts: +25% Convert Rate, +25% Capacity on the Mountain Top Field and +20% Tool Pollen! Now I'm going to fill this humidifier up see how it works! You keep on honeymaking cause this is the time of year to do it. Make sure you're using your planters and getting that Nectar. Nectar can increase your pollen and convert rate by double. Pay attention to the 5 different Nectar types and where to get them, and it'll make progressing way easier. And make sure to check out the Nectar Pot near the gate into this place. For 5 [Tickets], you can store 1 Nectar type of your choice. It'll be preserved untill you come back to claim it. [Tickets] are a bit easier to get now so I'd suggest using it. Dapper Bear's supposed to really emphasize this stuff, but somebody hasn't finished his quests yet... Anyway, thanks a lot! Merry Beesmas!   
Spirit Bear  | Mmmm...mhmm...(she's murmuring to herself with her eyes close)... _-Present Given-_ I've been counting [Snowflakes] all day as they fall onto the fields. (Yawn). Oh, it's quite hypnotic! I keep losing count because, well, it's like counting sheep. At one point, though, I counted. What was it... Over 50 [Snowflakes]? I lost count that time because of a Snow Storm. I mean, I can't keep up with that. ... Snow everywhere, like frosting on our Beesmas cake! Decorated with all those delightful contraptions this year. Like that wreath. Hehehh, Black Bear, bless his heart. Look at that Honey Wreath. I won't say more... Oh but Mother Bear's Gingerbread House! Oh, I like that one. ...(she's staring into the distance)... Is that a [Snowflake]? Or... Hmm. I'm tired of counting. I'm bored now. Why bother with something so mundane, in the most exciting time of the year! Bee Bear, oh that precious fool. Don't tell him I called him a fool. Not that he would mind. To think that we mere mortal bears could offend a honeyday deity. Anyways, that sweet, jovial bear, he brightens up the entire mountain in the darkest days of the year. How did we even make it through the winters before him? Well, I mean, I do remember... It wasn't so bad. We just celebrated, we just didn't focus so much on the bees. Not that you bees don't deserve it! Bee Bear is completely right about that. We celebrated the passing of the year in a more general sense. We still ate a lot of the same food, did similar customs, it just felt a little more... off the cusp. We still put ornaments on a tree, you see, we just didn't have a special name for it. Or did we? Hmmm... Was it... Oh, like I'll ever remember. Mother always had a tree, and we dolled it up for winter. Left it up much longer than we probably should have, we were never... punctual. But it was always so, so full of ornaments! Everyone was jealous of our tree. Perhaps the only time our hoarding tendencies were a source of envy. Hah! And things really took off as Beesmas became more and more of a thing. Mother couldn't help herself, any time she saw an ornament on sale. Mmhmm. And some of those - completely unnecessary, Mother. The only thing more imposing than our tree was the size of our debt. How did we even get approved for so many credit cards? Yaach... But it was worth it. You can't put a price on Beesmas Magic. Well, you can, sort of. Just take a look on Bee Bear's Catalog. But you understand what I mean. Those debts are long gone, and here I am countless years later and still reminiscing about our trees. Mother's taste for ornaments - they were well considered. ...(she's staring off into the distance)... ...32... Or was it? Drats. I've lost count again. I can't help myself! I just see the [Snowflakes] and I just start... But it's too hard! And it's boring. Let's do something else. What have you come to see me about, anyhow, dear? What's that you're holding - it's for me? Oh! A gift! Will you open it for Spirit Bear? My hands are full, you know. And sore. ...(You open the present for Spirit Bear)... ...(krumple krumple)... Oh no! Oh no no no! This - this is just too much! It's a... picture frame? With no picture. ... A what now? It's electronic? Oh dear you know I'm no good with electronics. Is this a television? No? What? A digital picture frame? The only digits I understand are the 10 on my paws, dear. I appreciate it, I do! I'll try my best to get some use out of it. I could just, tape a photo on it. Nooo... I'll do it. I'll figure it out. Thank you, you're so sweet. ... OK, goodbye now. ... Oh! YOUR gift! Yes yes I have one for you! A few things, actually. Including my [Memory Match Ornament]! With that on the tree, well, I don't quite recall what it does. You can check the tree yourself. (Yawn). First, why not check out that strange [Snowflake] in the Dandelion Field. Merry Beesmas, dear!   
Honey Bee  | Bzz zzz zzuh... _-Present Given-_ Bzzz buz ZZ! ZZZZZ BZZ BZZZZ!!! ...(krumple krumple)... (You open up the [Present] for Honey Bee) (The box is filled to the brim with honey) BUZZZZZZZUH BZZZZZZZ! Bzz zz zz! (Honey Bee's eye open wide with joy) (You can tell it's very grateful) Bzzzz zzz buzz bz BUZ zz. Gifted Bucko Bee  |  I'm just happy it's Winter. _-Present Given-_ Wait, don't tell me you've brought a [Present]? Of all the beekeepers I've met, you really are the coolest! One second, let me just try to unwrap this with my wings somehow... ...(krumple krumple)... Oh, it's a [Snow Tiara] Beequip! Well, this is a little awkward... Bucko Bees can't equip [Snow Tiaras]. But don't worry. I've got plenty of blue bee friends here at the HQ who will make great use out of this! Thanks! And now some gifts for you, including the [Snowman Ornament]! With that on the Beesmas Tree, you'll receive the following boosts: (+25% Convert Rate) (+25% Capacity in the Blue Flower Field and Pine Tree Forest) and (+10% Blue Field Capacity!) Hope that helps! Merry blue Beesmas!   
Gifted Riley Bee  |  Bucko Bee better not use that Snow Machine! It's TOO DANG COLD! _-Present Given-_ NUH-UH! You didn’t! You DID! Let me open it, let me open it! ...(krumple krumple)...Whaaat? It’s a [Dark Scythe]!!!! How on earth did you afford this? Do you realize how rare these are? We’ve only got 1 in the whole HQ! Man, now I feel silly. I got you a gift too, but it’s not even in the same league. Hope you’ll like it anyways. It includes the [Electric Candle Ornament]! With that on the Beesmas Tree, you’ll receive these boosts: +25% Convert Rate +25% Capacity in the Rose and Mushroom Field and +10% Pollen from Flames! Merry Beesmas! Now if you’ll excuse me, I’ve gotta setup my bPad, oh boy oh boy.   
Stick Bug  | You beekeepers are funny with your seasonal traditions. _-Present Given-_ I won't judge though. We all move to our own beat. So, let's see what you've brought me. ...(krumple krumple)... It's a gamelan! A traditional Indonesian percussive instrument. I've always appreciated the far-out hypnotic beats you can accomplish with these things. I really appreciate it and I'll put it to good use. But that'll have to wait.   
Gummy Bear  | A beekeeper approaches, Gummy Bee! -_If the player does not meet the requirements-_ A box wrapped in colorful paper... a gift? You believe you're worthy of exchanging gifts with US? HAH We do not accept trifles from meager meat-based civilians. Gummy Bee and I permit you to open it for us. ...(krumple krumple)... OH! You've brought live ammunition. A case of hand GOO-nades! How explosively sweet. I will put these to good use gumming up a village or two. KABOOM! In exchange, my gooey grunt, I give you this: The [Gummy Globe Ornament]! Let it serve as a constant reminder that better days WILL COME. And the unbearably bitter and salty states of all worlds will give way to a SWEET future. ALL worlds will be GOOnited when Gummy Bee and I are through. And with that ornament on your wooden plant, you'll receive the following boosts to aid in our GOO CRUCADE. +10% Capacity +25% Coconut Field Capacity +1% Critical Chance And +10% Goo! Now go GOO UP the pathetic Beesmas festivities once and for all!   
Bubble Bee Man  | 😐 _-If the player does not meet the requirements-_ Ooooh... uh... Ok, well. Yeah, first, thanks. But uhhh no thanks. No. Just no. I mean, er... ![Hivesticker eviction](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D) | **This piece of content goes bye bye.** The following content has been removed from the game. The contents below may be archival, but feel free to edit below.  
---|---  
  
Presents returned in Beesmas 2020. Presents functioned the same as in Beesmas 2018, except with additional NPCs able to receive presents. Presents were the only way to obtain [Ornaments](/wiki/Ornaments "Ornaments") from NPCs. They were also needed to be delivered to do some quests from [Bee Bear](/wiki/Bee_Bear "Bee Bear"). 

## Ways to obtain

  * Multiple quests from Bee Bear awarded a total of ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)10 **Presents** , along with other rewards.
  * [Bee Bear’s Catalog](/wiki/Bee_Bear%E2%80%99s_Catalog "Bee Bear’s Catalog") sold ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 **Present** for ![Snowflake](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)250 [Snowflakes](/wiki/Snowflake "Snowflake") and ![Gingerbread Bear](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)10 [Gingerbread Bears](/wiki/Gingerbread_Bear "Gingerbread Bear").
  * [Onett](/wiki/Onett "Onett") gave ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 **Present** (along with other items) upon claiming a hive for the first time during the event's period.
  * Donating ![Strawberry](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)[Strawberries](/wiki/Strawberry "Strawberry") to the [Galentine Shrine](/wiki/Wind_Shrine#Galentine_Shrine "Wind Shrine") had a chance to give ![Present](data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D)1 **Present** , along with other rewards.
